## Kollu Tünel Acma Makinelerinin Performans Tahmini icin Ampirik ve Yapay Sinir Agi Modellerinin Karsilastirilmasi

Comparison of the Empirical and Artificial Neural Network Models to Estimate Performance of Roadheaders

## D.Tumac,E.Avunduk

istanbul Teknikniversitesi,Maden Muhendisligi Dlm,34469 Maslak-lstanbul

OZET Kollu tünel acma makineleri (Roadheaders) madencilik ve insaat sektorlerinde tünel ve galeri acmak icin kullanilan makinelerdir biridir.Formasyonlarin jeolojik ve geoteknik ozellikleri bumakinelerin kullaniminiönemli olcüdesinirlanmaktir.Bu makinelerin secim kriterleri tinel kazisinim verimliligi icin cok önemlidir.Omeginsecilen makine gecilecek formasyon icin uygun degilse,gunluk ilerleme hizlarr aniden düser ve böylelikle butn tinelcilik faaliyetleri olumsuz olarak etkilenir.Bu olumsuz faktorler bircok arastirmaciyi makine secimi ve hizli kazi sistemlerinde performansin önceden tahmin edilebilmesi icin bilimsel calismalar uygulamaya sevk etmistir.Arasturmacilar ayrica kollu tinel acma makinelerinin performans tahminine yonelik model gelistirme calismalarina da odaklanmistir.

Bu calisma kapsaminda,STFA firmasi tarafindan insasi yapilan Kücuksu atiksu tinclinde kullanilan kollu tinel acma makinesinin performansi incelenmistir. Bu atiksu tüneli istanbul'un Avrupa\_yakasinda Kücuksu'danHekimbasmakadaruzanmaktadir.Calismaninamacina ulasabilmek icin ilk öncegecilen formasyonlarin jeolojik ve geoteknik özellikleri tayin edilmis sonra ise kollu tunel acma makineleri icin gelistirilmis olan bazi ampirik performans tahmin modelleri test edilmistir.Bu amac icin basinc dayanimi (UCS ve kaya kalite gostergesi (RQD degerleri bu modellerde girdi verisi olarak kullanilmistir. Gecilen formasyon icin arazide lculen ile ampirik modellerden tahmin edilen anlik kazi hizi (ICR degerleri karsilastirilmistr. Calismanin sonunda,UCS,RQD ve arazi ICR degerlerini iceren bir veri seti olusturulmus ve kullanilmistir.Elde edilen sonuclar daha onceden gelistirilmis olan ampirik modeller ile irdelenmistir.Bu calisma performans tahmini icin YSA'nin ampirik modellerden daha iyi sonuc verdigini gostermistir.

ABSTRACT Roadhcaders arc onc of thc main cutting machines for rapid and cfficicnt tunnel and roadway drivages.On the other hand,the applications of thesc machines are limited to the geology and geotechnical properties of rock formations. The selection criteria of roadheaders is vital for tunnelling activities.For example,if the selected roadheader is not proper for the rock formations,\_daily advance rateswhichaffectthe wholetunnellingfacilitiesdecrease dramatically.These facts lead many researchers to carry out scientific research programs in order to increase the performance of the rapid excavations systems.During the last decades, researchers have also focused on developing performance prediction models for roadheaders.

The performanceof a roadheader used for excavation of Kucuksu seweragetunnel constructed by STFA was s investigated. Kucuksu sewerage tunnel driven from Kucuksu to Hekimbasi is located in Anatolian part of Istanbul. In order to achieve the main objective of this study,firstly, the geomechanical properties of intact rock and rock mass properties of the tunnel zones are found out,then,the in-situ performance data of roadheader are recorded. Secondly, the uniaxial compressive strength UCS and rock quality designationRQD are used as input parameters in the previously developed empirical models in order to understand the efficiency of these models. The relationships between measured and predicted instantaneous cutting rate ICR are determined for different encountered geological formations.Finally,a data set including UCS,RQD,and measured ICR are established.Artificial Neural Network ANN technique is used for predicting of the ICR of the roadheader.Three different empirical models are compared to the ANN model. Mean squared.errors and coefficients of determination of these three methods are recorded and compared with those obtained with ANN.It is concluded that the artificial neural network designed in this study draws a better performance than the empirical models.This paper demonstrated promising results in predicting the achieved instantaneous net cutting rate (ICR for the case study in question.

## 1 GIRI$

Buyuk sehirlerdeki hizli nufus artisi yer alti yapilarinaolan gereksinimi artturmaktadir. Böylelikle,hili kaz teknolojilerine olan talep ise gittikce artmaktadir.Kollu tunel acma makineleri ilk yatirim maliyeti avantajlarindan otürü yumusak ve orta sert formasyonlarin kazisindaki kullanimlari gittikce artmaktadir Copur et al.1998.

Kollu tuinel acma makinelerinin basarili bir sekilde kullanim formasyona uygun makine secimi, keski tuketimi ve üretim hizi tahmini ile dogruorantilidir.Bumakinclerin performansianlik kazi hizi ICRtahmini olarak adlandirilir ve m/h birimi ile ifade edilmektedir.ICRkaya parametreleri, makine özellikleri ve isletme parametreleri ile dogrudan iliskilidirRostami1994.Kollu tünel acma makineleri icin literatürde bugune kadar bir cokampirikperformans tahmin modeli gelistirilmistir fakat bu modeller gercek arazi verileriyle ya zayif ya da orta dereceli iliskiler vermektedir.

Yapay sinir aglarinn YSA) madencilik ve tinelcilik sektörlerindeki prohlemlerin cozimü icinkullanim gun gectikce artmaktadir.Singhvd.2001,Khandelwal vd.2004,Bilgin vd.2006,Kahraman vd. 2006,Yilmaz ve Yuksek 2008,Tiryaki 2009,Dehghanvd.2010,Manouchehrian vd.2010,Ceryan vd.2012,Ocak ve Seker 2012,Enayatollahi vd.2013 yapay sinir aglarini bu amac icin kullanan arastirmacilardan sadece bir kacidir.

Bu calisma kollu tinel acma makinelerinin performans tahmini icin yapay sinir aglarinin nasil kullanilabilecegini ozetlemektedir.

## 2 KOLLU TONELACMA MAKINELERiNINPERFORMANS TAHMiNi iCiN GELiSTiRILMIS BAZI MODELLER

Kollu tunel acma makinelerinin performans tahmininde genel olarak iki ana yontem bulunmaktadir.Birinciyöntemlaboratuar ortaminda performans tahmini icin kücuk veya buyük ölcekli kazi deneyleridir.Ikinci yontem isearazide toplanan formasyon ve makineperformansverilerikullanlarak gelistirilmis olan ampirik modellerdir.

Literatürde en cok kabul goren performans tahminyontemlerinden biri Rostami vd. 1994 tarafindan gelistirilmistir.Bu modelde birim hacimdeki kayaci kesmek icin harcanan spesifik enerji SE miktari, kollu tunel acma makinesinin kesici kafa gucunu P ve enerji transferkatsayisini kkullanlmaktadtr (Esitlik1.

<!-- formula-not-decoded -->

buradaICRanlik kazi hizim/h,Pkesici kafagucu kW SEspesifik enerji kWh/m²,kenerji transfer katsayisi 0,45. Copurvd.2001 tam boyutlu dogrusal kazi deneylerinde optimum kosullarda elde edilen spesifik enerji degerlerinin kayaclarin basing ve cekme dayanimi degerlerleriile iliskilerinin oldugu vurgulamislardir.Ayrica, bu deney düzeneginin performans tahmini icin en guvenilir yontem oldugunu belirtmislerdir.

Ampirik performanstahminmodelleri özellikle gecmis tecrubelere ve araziden veri toplama ile yapilanistatistikselcalismalar dayanmaktadir.Modellerin guvenilirligi veri toplama kalitesine, veri cesitliligine ve veri coklugunadayanmaktadir.Bilgin vd.1988, 1990,1996,1997,2004kollu tunelacma makineleri icin en yayin olarak kullanilan ampirik performans tahmin modelini gelistirmislerdir (Esitlik 2-3).

## 3 YAPAY SiNIR AGLARI

Yapay sinir aglari sistem tanimada ve lineer olmayan sistemlerin modellenmesinde kullanilmak üzerebiyolojik nöronlardan esinlenerek gelistirilmis karmasik matematiksel modellerdir.Bir yapay sinir ag genellikle giris katmani,ara katman ya da katmanlar ve cikis katmanindan olusur.Her katman cok sayida noron icerir ve noron sayisi kullanici tarafindan problemin olcegine gorebelirlenir.Biyolojiksinir sistemine benzer olarak tüm noronlar birbirlerine baglidirOstem,2006).

Bir yapay sinir agi sistemle ilgili herhangi bir fiziksel bilgi icermez,ancak sistemin giris ve cikis verilerine gore egitilerek bu veriler arasinda bir iliski kurabilir.Bu amacla yapay sinir agi Örnek bir veri kumesi kullanilarak egitilmelidir.Basarh biryapay sinir ag egitmedogrulama ve test olmak zere uc asamasonucundaelde edilir.Bu nedenle ornek veri kümesi rastgele üc alt kümeye ayristirilir... Genelliklebunlardan birinin egitim icin kullanlacak olanmn eleman sayisi digerlerinden daha fazladur.Diger iki kume ise dogrulama ye test icin kullanilr. $ekil 1de bir noron detayli olarak verilmistir Graupe 1997Ostem 2006.

Boyle yapay sinir aglarina ileri yonl (geri yayilmaliyapay sinir agi denir.Agurlik katsayilari belirlenen ögrenme a algoritmasina gore guncellenir.Ogrenme algoritmasi gercek cikis degeri ile tahmin edilen cikis degeri arasindaki hatayi minimize etmeye calisir. Ogrenmealgoritmasi olarak azalan gradyan yontemleri i yaygn olarakkullamhr.Bu yöntemler arasinda cogunlukla LevenbergMarquardtalgoritmasthizhvegivenilir olmasi nedeniyle kullanilir.Hata icin de özel bir olcut belirlenebilir ancak yapay sinir aginin egitilmesinde siklikla toplam karesel hata kullanilir, Agurlik katsayilarinin guncellenmesinden sonra sürec bir sonraki giris-cikis veri grubu icin tekrarlanir.Tüm veri kumesinin bir ogrenme cevrimine devir epochadi verilir.

Pek cok uygulamada yapay sinir agi bir veya daha fazla ara katman ve her katmanda birden fazla noron icerir.Bu durumda geri yayilma algoritmasi butun mimariye nüfuz ederek bütun katmanlardaki butün noronlar arasindakiagirlikkatsayilarin günceller. Onek bir yapay sinir ag $ekil 2'de verilmistir.

## The project TEO "TunnelEmisor Oriente" in Mexico City

E.Dal Negro,A.Altuna,S.Acarkan

MAPEl,Underground Technology Team International,Milan ltaly

ABSTRACT The paperg gives firstly a general presentation about the project TEO,currently under excavation in Mexico City.Secondly,the tunnel Section included between the Shafts 5 and 6 is approached more in details.In particular,thc TBM cxcavating such a section includes the extraction of the muck with the use of a solid matter pump.The pump works properly and with acceptable pressures only when high amounts of water are added to the muck. In order to avoid so high consumptions of water (that lead to several problems from economic and social points of view),a solution was found with the injection of a high-lubricating polymer, diluted in water at very low concentration before its use.

## 1 INTRODUCTION

The project TEO (Tunnel Emisor Oriente) is currently one ofthegreatestprojects worldwide.The TEO is under construction in Mexico City and deals with the construction of anew underground hydraulic channel 7.00 meter diameter with sheathing,which will be used to collect the wastewaters in the Mexican megalopolis and to bring them to a treatment plant located out of the town.

Once completed,this tunnel will play an important role in order to solve some of the worst problems of Mexico Citymanage of wastewaters, risks offlooding (that periodically hitthe town) and..soil subsidence.The safety and quality of life of people living in Mexico City and its suburbs (more than 20 millions will therefore be significantly improved.

CONAGUAComisionNacional del Agua,the Mexican Commission responsible for the watermanagement assignedthe construction of the project TEO to the Joint VentureCOMISSA, consisting ofthe Mexican contractors:ICA,Carso,Cotrisa Lombardo and Estrella.

The whole project is divided in several contracts:

- Section 1:originally it was between the Shaft 0,located in the north-east of Mexico City,and the Shaft 5,for a total of approx. 10 km.An inundation hit the working shaft and damagedtheTBMseriously,thus stopping it for a rather long period.For this reason,another TBM was launched from the Shaft 5 in order to accelerate the completion of this first Section. It is currently excavating towards the Shaft named 3A.
- Section 2:from the Shaft 5 to the Shaft 10,for a total of 11.5 km.
- Section 3:from the Shaft 10 to the Shaft 13.
- Section 4:from the Shaft 17 to the Shaft : 13.
- Section 5:from the Shaft 20 to the Shaft 17.
- Scction 6:from the Exit Portal to the Shaft20.

TheJoint Venture COMISSA is excavating the 62km long tunnel with a total of 6TBM,3 manufactured by the German Herrenknccht and3by American Robbins.

The geology found by the 6 TBM during the 62 km of tunnel is very heterogeneous,as easily predictable for such a long tunnel.For thisreason, theTBMhave aflexible configuration of the cutter head that allows to change the excavation tools according to the geological variations of the ground.

The soil conditions includemainly clay and silts especially in the first sections limestone and sandstones. intercalated with basaltic rocks.

Underground water is normally not a significant problem relating to the control of EPB pressures.Up to now,only in the Section 6 very high pressures of water were found due to the high overburdens,up to 6.57.0barst thus representing an important problem for the TBM advance and the tunnel waterproofing.ATBMcould advance extremely slowly for several wecks because of such water pressures.Finally the injection of a super-absorbing polymer was able to solve the problem and to allow the control of the EPB pressures and the extraction of muck through the screw conveyor.

## 2SECTION1,SHAFT5-SHAFT6

The Shaft 5 is located in the suburbs of Mexico City and from this position two TBM were assembled and driven by the Mexican Contractor ICA:

- AHerrenknecht EPB,excavating from the Shaft 5 to the Shaft6
- -ARobbins EPB,cxcavating from the Shaft 5 to the Shaft 3A

Inparticular the 1.4km-long section between Shafts 5 and 6 will be considered in the following part of the paper.

- -The first part of the tunnel is mainly includes a high percentage of fine material, in particular clay with intercalation of silt and fine sand

Several soil cores were extracted before the TRM launch. Their analysis allowed to draw the geological profile below, that can be summarized as following;

- In the second part the geology switches to a higher percentage of fine and mediumcoarse sand.

## 2.1 Ground Conditioning

As already known,the conditioning of the soil to be excavated with EPB represents one of the most important aspects in order to achieve successful TBM productions.

The important steps to be considered for getting good results regarding the.soil conditioning are:

Therefore the proper choice ofthe productsfoaming agents and/or polymers and their parameters has to be carefully analyzed by the Contractor.

- -Prior to thc TBM launch:
- to accumulate information about the - tunnel geology: geological profile, geotechnical characterization, other tunnels excavated nearby, etc.
- During the TBM advance: -
- to collect a representative sample of soil and to carry out tests in the laboratory.
- to optimize thewhole ground conditioning system according to the local situation TBM size,distribution of the injection lines,variations of the geological conditions, etc.

The samples showed quite high characteristics ofstickiness that. can represent a problem during the TBM advance. For this reason it is necessary to add a rather high amount of liquid foam.

Regardingthetunnel sectionbetween Shafts 5 and 6,beforc thc TBM launch somc samples of soil were collected from the TEO project and sent to the MAPEI Laboratory located at the Technical University of Turin. Such a laboratory is fully dedicated to tests for ground conditioning.

Thc tests proved that a foaming agent in combination withalubricating polymer (POLYFOAMER FP/CCgives better results than the use of a foaming agent combined with a dispersing agent sometimes defined as anti-clay agent.Thisbecausethe lubricating polymer is able toencapsulate" the clay particles and stop their water absorption. This effectis much more immediate than the dispersing effect given by the other type of polymerFig.1

The aspects considered by the Contractor to check the suitability of ground conditioning were:

- 3.Advance speed (the average values has been between 30 and35mm/min

## 2.2 The Solid Matter Pump

The TBM are configured with a particular design that allows the extraction of the excavated soil in two ways:

- -With a solid matter pump
- -With a belt conveyor discharging in a train wagon

The two systems are exchangeable, thus making the TBM more flexible depending on the punctual geological conditions, but the pump is the system generally used by the Contractor to extract the soil from the tunnel.

The ground conditioning parameters found with the laboratory tests had to be controlled and optimized during the TBM advance.In particular,the extraction of muck made with the.solid ..matter..pump influenced significantly the ground conditioning system and represents a factor which could not be simulated with the traditional laboratory tests (Fig.2).

<!-- image -->

Photograph

Figure 2.Schwing pumps TAP-140 used in the Herrenknecht TBM

<!-- image -->

Photograph

The solid matter pump is placed immediately after the screw conveyor and is especially designed for pumping cohesive soil with a high water content.It is therefore needed to add a significant amount of liquid in the soil in order to facilitate its pumping operations until the tunnel exit.In several rings,the amount of water added was almost 100%of the volume of soil excavated.

This important addition of pure water leads to two problems:

- In the area of Mexico City,water is a precious resource and cannot be constantly usedinso huge amountsfor  tunnel constructions.
- -The excavated soil stocked at site has to be transported away by trucks.The number of trucks needed increase significantly if so many liters of water are injected per advance, thus making the total costs for the Contractor higher.

For these reasons,it was necessary to find anothcr way how to facilitate the soil pumping.

Its.use allows tolubricate theclay superficially clay and silt are therefore

The solution found is to combine the use of a foaming agent with the injection of the lubricating polymer MAPEDRILL M1.This polymer is diluted in water at very low concentration before use and then injected into the excavation chamber and at the end of the screw conveyor through a dedicated line.

facilitated to slide onto the metallic pipes between the pump and the tunnel exit.In this way, the pumping pressures are reduced and at the same time it can be decreased the total amount of water added for pumping the soil.

solution helped solve the two main problems that arise from the use of the solid matter pump that means to decrease significantly the amount of water needed per advance and to avoid too high pumping pressure.

The amount of polymer and fluid used per ring was varying according to:

The polymer MAPEDRILL M1 was used with continuity in approx.450 rings during the drilling in clay. The average amount of polymer injected per ring was around 10 kg. The injection of fluid decreased significantly down to 5000=10000 liters per ring,that means in average half than before without the addition of the lubricating polymer.

The solid matter pump certainly represents a solution alternative to the more classic belt conveyor, useful for example where the lack of space in the TBM starting station or shaft avoids the installation of a belt conveyor.In such a case, the pump is able to ensure higher production than the use of muck removal cars and trains,because it is a faster and more constant system.

- -The geological variations

The soil was pumped from the TBM to the Shaft 5for the first 450 rings that means 675 m.After this progressivean exit for the pumping lines was created from the tunnel crown to the surface.

- -The pumping distance

## 3 CONCLUSIONS

TheTEO representsone ofthemost challenging undergroundprojects inthe world,with its more than 60 km length and the extremely varying geological conditions.

In order to allow the pumping of different types of soil for long distances (up to 675 m) andwithout achicvingtoo highand dangerous pumping pressures,firstly huge amounts of water were injected per advance. This was creating problems in the muck removal from the joh-site (several trncks had to be used, with consequent increases of costs and logistic problems and in water supplying to the job-site.

In particular,the section between the Shafts 5 and 6 was excavated with an EPB shield and used an innovative way to extract the soil out of the tunnel that means with a solid matter pump.

A solution was found by creatinga lubricatingsolution injectedinto the excavation chamber and the screw conveyor. Such a solution was generated by adding a polymer to the pure water,able to lubricate the clay particles superficially and to help their flow inside the pumping line. This As the TEO project in Mexico City proves, the choice of the right product helps significantly solve some of the problems which can be related to the use of such a technology for the muck removal.

The use of a pump represents an important factor which has to be considered while evaluating the most suitable products and parameters for ground conditioning.Specific site tests need to be carried out in order to find the optimal solution.

## Bir Atiksu Tünelinde TBM Kaynakli Yeryüzü Titresimlerinin Olcümü i ve Analizi

Measurement and Analysis of Surface Vibrations Induced by TBMon A Wastewater Tunnel

## A.Sakcali

Sleyman Demirel Universitesi, Mühendislik Fakiltesi,Maden Mühendisligi Bolimii, lsparta

## U.Ozer,A.Karadogan

Istanbul Universitesi, Muhendislik Fakiltesi, Maden Muhendisligi Bolim,lstanbul

## M.Akgul,Z.Guclcan,S.Meric

NTF Insaat Ticaret Ltd.$ti.

## T.Mungan

Yertas Ins.Turz.Maden Ltd.Sti.

ÖZET Dnyada genel nifus artisina paralel olarak sehirlesme hizla artmakta, bunun sonucunda da kentlerde ulasim problemleri ortaya cikmaktadir.Yeralti yapilarinin artmasiyla ulasimin hizlanacagi asikardir.Buyük nfuslu sehir merkezlerinde yeralt yapilarinin yapimu patlatmal kazi yaninda mekanize kazi sistemlerini de teknik ve ekonomik yonden önemli konuma getirmistir.Minimum dizeyde olsa bile,patlatmali kazida oldugu gibi mekanize kazinin da cevreye\_verecegi etki soz konusudur.

Bu calismada,tam cephe tünel acma makinesi ile yapilan bir tunel kazisinin yeryuzunde olusturdugu titresim etkileri incelenmistir.Patlatma kaynakli titresim olcümu icin de kullanilan uygun bir titresim olcum cihazi kullanilms ve enine,boyuna ve düsey bilesenler olmak üzere üc parcacik hizi ile kesici kafa torku arasindaki iliski incelenmistir.

increasing general population and as a result of this,transport problems in urban arise. Transportation will accelerate with the increasing of underground structures. The construction of underground structures in overpopulated city center using mechanized excavation system besides blasting excavation brought importance position technically and economically.Even though a minimum level, there is the environmental impact of mechanized excavation like the blasting excavation.

In this study,the impact on the earth of a tunnel excavation made with a full face tunnel horing machine\_(TBM) was examined.Asuitable vibration measurementdevice used for measuring the vibration from blasting was used and the three-component recordings including transverse,lengthwise,and vertical component were evaluated.The relation between maximum particle velocity on the surface and cutter hcad torque depending on the horizontal distance was examined.

## 1TAM CEPHE TONEL ACMA MAKiNESI(TBM)

Madencilik ve insaat sektorünün teknolojik gelisimiyle birlikte yer altr yapilarina verilen onem gün gectikce artmaktadir.Yerlesim merkezlerinde acilan 1 tunellerin ac-kapa gibi maliyetin az fakat cevresel etkinin fazla oldugu yontemler kullanilarak degil de ilk yatirim maliyeti yuksek fakat cevresel etkinin az oldugu yöntemlerle yapilmasinin gerekliligi aciktir.ilk yatirim maliyeti yuksek olmasina ragmen tam cephe tünel acma makineleri TBMtasmangibi istenmeyenyeralti hareketlerinin onlenmesi, daha.sessiz, titresimsiz ve hizli calismasi nedeniyle tercih edilen kazi makineleri haline gelmistir. Sert, orta sert,yumusak ve akici formasyonlar icin kullanilacak kafa dizaynlari ve keski tipleri, makineyi dengeleme sistemleri, tahkimat sistemleri,cikarilan pasayi tasima sistemleri cesitli yönlerden farkliliklar göstermektedir. Bu nedenle,acilacak yeralti boslugu boyunca gecilecekformasyonlarintespitedilmesi kullanilacak makinenin seciminde önemli bir etmendirCinar ve Feridunoglu,2002).

- Kollu.Galeri Acma Makineleri (Roadheader)

Tunelacmamakineleri acilacakolan zeminin özelliklerinekayacÖzelliklerine veya zeminin su icerigine,kazi performansi gibi degisik parametrelere gore asagidaki gibi siniflandirilir,

- ZirhliKollu Galeri Acma Makineleri (Roadheader Shield)
- Zirhsiz Kollu Galeri Acma Makineleri (Roadheader Open)
- Tam Cephe Tunel Acma Makineleri (Full Face)

- Tam Cephe Tek Zirhli (Full Face Single . Shield)

- Boru itme Sistemiyle Calisan Tünel Acma Makineleri (Pipe Jacking (Muarat,2004.

Tam cephe tunel acma makineleriTBM teknolojik gelismeler parelelinde hertürlü zemin ve kaya sartlarinda kazinin yapilabilecegi duruma gelmistir. Tam cephe tinel acma makineleri aynay keskileri ile tamamen kavrayarak kazi yapar. Kesici kafa aynaya dogru itilir ve bu itilme sirasinda kafanin dönmesi kazi olayini gerceklestirir.

Aynadan kazilan malzeme kesici kafa üzerinde bulunan kanatciklarlakesici kafa arkasindaki bolüme aktarilir.Burada bulunan konveyor vasitasiyla cikan pasanin nakliyati yapilir.Aynaninkesici kafa tarafindan tamamenkapatilmasiaynayagecilmesini engelledigi icinkeskilerin degistirilmesi zordur.Bugibidurumlardatinelacma makinesinin parelelinde kesici kafanin onine dogru galeri acilarak keskiler kontrol edebilir (Cinar ve Feridunoglu,2002).

## 2TiTRESIM OLUSUMU VE OLCUMO

Kayacin icindeilerleyenmekanize kazi makinelerinin ve patlatmali kazininyarattigi sismik dalgalar kaya ortaminda bir noktadan bir noktaya ulasan enerji transferini temsil etmektedir. ilk basta ortama yeni giren enerji, ortamdakidenge konumunu bozarakyer degistirmeye neden olmaktadir.Eger,ortam yeni gelen enerjiye elastik özellik gostermezse,enerji sonumlemekte ve sadece titresimiazalmisdalgalaryansimaktadir. Elastiközellik gosterdiginde isebozulan ortamin sonucu olarak komsu ortamlar denge konumundan aynlarak yay-agirlik mekanizmasina benzer bir sekilde salinim meydanagetirmektedir.Böylecebozulan ortamin her elementi,salinimin özelliklerini diger elementlere de gecirerek ortamda dalga hareketi olusturmaktadir (Kahriman,2007).

Olcum aletinin veri alma genligi 2 saniye ve minimum PPV sin 0,04mm/sn olarak ayarlanmistir.TBM'in gectigi koordinatlarin yzeydeki en yakin koordinatlarda, TBM kazi durumundaykentitresimverilerialinmaya baslar.Veriler Blastware tabanli bilgisayar paket programina aktarilir.

Olcumler Sekil 1'de gosterilen titresim lcim cihaziyla alinmstir.Titresimolcum cihazinin calisma prensibibirbirine dik yerlestirilmis 3 adet jeofonun x, y ve z eksenlerindeki maksimum parcacik hizlarini ve bunlarin frekanslarini ölcmeye dayalidir. Bu cihazda maksimum parcacik hizi PPV icinmm/snfrekansicinHz birimleri kullanilmaktadir(Kahriman,2007).

## 5SONUCLAR

TBM ile olcum cihazi arasindaki mesafenin yüksek oldugu yerlerde ölcüm alinamamis,bu mesafenin 15 m'nin altina düstügü noktalarda olcumler alinmustir.Yaniyuksekdüsey uzakhikl mesafelerdeTBMinyeryuzune etkisi yok denecek kadar azdir.

TBM'in yeryuzüne olan titresiminin etkisi incelenmistir. Tüm olcümler birlikte kullanilarak yapilananalizlerdebir iliski bulunamamisve bu olcümler yatay mesafelere bagli olarak siniflandirilarak degerlendirilmistir.

Yeryuzüne cok yakin mesafelerde TBM'in kesici kafa basmci (buna bagli olarakta torku ile maksimum parcacik hizi arasindaters orantilibiriliskivardir.Yaniyakin mesafelerde TBM'in yeryuzüne olan etkisini azaltmak icin TBM kcsici kafasinin torkunu artirmak gerekecektir.Uzak mesafelerde ise TBM kesici kafasinin torku ile maksimum parcacikhizi dogru orantilolarak degistigindenyeryuzu etkisinin azaltilmasi icin TBM torkunun azaltilmasi gerekmektedir.

Olctm alinan formasyon killi kirec tasi kumtasi olmasina ragmen zemin icerisinde bolgeselkalitedegisimi,torkun wve titresimlerinfarkldegerlerdeölculmesine sebep olmustur.Degerlendirmede elde edilen korelasyon katsayismin bu nedenle düsük oldugu tahmin edilmektedir.

## Rock Mass Classification Systems:Are They Applicable to Prediction of TBM Performance?

## J.Khademi Hamidi

Tarbiat Modares University,Tehran,Iran

## H.Bejari

Sistan and Balouchestan University,Zahedan,Iran

ABSTRACT Rock mass classification systems are often used in many empirical design practices in rock engineering contrasting with their original intent and applications,as for instance in estimation of TBM performance in various tunneling projects.However,parameters in these classifications were related to support design they were not selected to describe rock mass boreability.The results of many investigations s in this issue showed a weak correlation exists between TBM rate of penetration and rock mass classifications.This limitation was overcome by tuning up the rock mass classifications in this study.The influence of rock mass properties on TBM performance was studied and a new empirical equation was developed for predicting TBM performance by using rock mass fabric index of RMR system. The results of the analysis in this study revealed that the existing rock mass classification systems with the present scheme have limited accuracy in TBM performance prediction.

## 1INTRODUCTION

Since James Robbins developed his TBM in 1954,tunnel boring machines have become very versatile and used favorably in various ground conditions.Since that date,study and analysis of TBM performance have been ultimate. goals ofmanyresearchers.A comprehensive review of the literature on the subject can be found in Khademi Hamidi et al.,2010and Farrokh et al.,2012.

Inbrief,all theTBM.performance prediction models can be divided into two distinguished approaches,namely, theoretical and empirical ones.In a group of empirical modelsattemptswere made to correlate TBM performance to rock mass classifications.Among the most commonly used classification systems,Rock Mass Rating (RMR and Rock Mass Quality Index Q have been used more frequently in TBM performance prediction. There are,however, two questions regarding these systems when they are going to be used in TBM

performance prediction. Are these systems in their present tscheme applicable to performance prediction?Which performance parameter is it supposed to cover: penetration rate or advance rate?

This paper is a typical examplefor application of the rock mass classification systems developed for rock mass characterization and tunnel design in TBM performance prediction.

## 2 APPLICATIONOFROCKMASS CLASSIFICATION SYSTEMSINTBM PERFORMANCE PREDICTION

Rock mass classifications have been quite popular and mostly being used for preliminary design and planning purposes of mining and tunneling projects. According to Bieniawski (1989,a rock mass classification scheme is intended to classify the rock masses,provide abasis for estimating deformation aad strength properties,supply quantitative data for support estimation,and present a platform forcommunication between exploration, design and construction groups.Over the years,the application of early rock mass classifications have been extended for using in various purposes,for instance development of MRMR in mining and SMR in slope stability analysis from RMR systemand development of QrsM and RME in TBM performanceanalysis from Q and RMR systems,respectively.However,extensive studies have been carried out for estimating the TBM performance based on rock mass classification systems without any modification in them.

Sapigni et al,2002 correlated TBM performance parameters including penetration rate PR and FPI to RMR classification system using over 700 data sets from three TBMdriven tunnels mostlyhard metamorphic rocks.The study showed that TBM performance is maximum in tair rock mass conditionRMR=40-70) while slower penetration is experienced in both too bad and too good rock masses. Ribacchi and Lembo-Fazio (2005) correlatedTBM performance parameters including penetration and advance rate and specific penetration to tworockqualityindexesnamelyjoint spacingand RMRPi.e.modified RMR system with fixed rating value of 15 for groundwater and eliminated correction factors

Cassinelli et al.,1982used correlation between Rock Structure Rating (RSR) system and actual TBM performance to evaluate TBMpenetrationrate.TheRSRwas originally developed for the determination of the appropriate steel rib tunnel wall support. Afterwards,Innaurato et al.1991 modified Cassinellis1982,model by including the uniaxial compressive strength in the model. McFeat-Smith andAskilsrud1993and Grandori et al.,1995,provided a correlation between integrated mass system IMS and TBM utilization, penetration and advance rate in TBM driven tunnels in Hong Kong. Sundaram and Rafek1998 studied the correlations between five TBM operational parameters and sixteen rock and rock mass parameters in hard granitic rock and found a satisfactorycorrelation between Q system and TBM field penetration indexFPI.

related to joint orientation).They concluded that,even simple quality indexes,such as the partial rating of joint spacing in the RMR classification,has sound predictive value for penetration rate.Hassanpour et al.2010 developed empirical equations to estimate TBM field penetration indexFPI through the most common rock mass classifications including RMR,Q and GSI system.

In all these rock mass classification based prediction models,the obtained results were nearly similarrock mass quality in its both critical and extreme conditions,i.e.very poor and very good rock mass classes,does not facilitate the TBM performance including penetration and advance rate.Also the correlation between rock mass classes and TBM penetration rate is rather poor.These can be seen in Figure 1,which shows the correlation of TBM net penetration rate or penetration per cutterhead revolution (PRev with RMR and GSI systems on a database compiled from Zagros TBM driven tunnel. The database is described in detail in KhademiHamidietal.,2010.Also, relationship between TBM rate of penetration ROPand RMR on the database of Gilgel Gibe II tunnel is illustrated in Figure 2.

As it can be followed from Figures 1 and 2, the coefficients of determination R2are rather low and are,subsequently,of limited practical use for the prediction of TBM penetration rate.Suitability of the existing rock mass classification systems for TBM performance prediction is illustrated by RMR system in the following section.

## 3 PARAMETERSINVOLVEDINRMR SYSTEM FORTBM PERFORMANCE

The RMR system,otherwise known as the Geomechanics Classification,was developed by Bieniawski 1973.It was modified over the years as more case histories became available and to conform with international standard andprocedures. Sixparameters including uniaxial compressive strength of rock material UCS),RQD,joint spacing, joint condition,groundwater conditions and joint orientation are used to classify a rock mass using the RMR system.

Figure 1.Relationship between TBM net penetration rate and two rock mass classifications(Zagros tunnel)

<!-- image -->

Scatter plot

Figure 2.Relationship between TBM rate of penetration and RMR (Gilgel Gibe II tunnel)

<!-- image -->

Line chart

Each of these parameters is classified and each class given a value or rating to express its influence on tunnel stability.However, there may be relationship between the system parameters and other variables such as TBM penetration rate.

Figures 3 and 4 show the correlation of TBM FPI with RMR five input parameters basic RMR.and RMR on the data taken from Zagros tunnel.

Figure 3.Correlation of FPI with RMR input parameters (Zagros tunnel)

<!-- image -->

Table

Figure 4.Correlation of FPI with RMR and Basic RMR (Zagros tunnel)

<!-- image -->

Scatter plot

Since the parameters joint condition (JC and groundwater condition. (GW) inRMR systems are qualitativedescriptive ones,the partial ratings of these parameters are used in this analysis.As seen in the figuresthe UCS shows the highest Rvalue,0.70.The R value decreases in the order of the joint spacing0.62,RQD0.59,partial rating of joint conditions 0.45 and partial rating of groundwater conditions 0.04.Comparison of the R values between the correlation of FPI with RMR and basic RMR indicates that the adjustment factor for joint orientation in RMR system has negligible effect on TBM FPI.Also,correlation between FPI and UCS as an input paramctcr of RMR system has a more coefficient of determinationR=0.70 than that for basic RMR or RMRR2=0.68. In other words,the bivariate analysis shows that UCS by itself accounts for 70% of the variation ofthe FPI.Addingfourmore parameters led to a marginal decrease in R from0.7 to 0.68.This means that the effect of UCS on correlation cocfficient is overshadowed by the additional parameters of RMR system.

Itis obvious that the use of a single variablcofRMRwill providea simple predictivemodel ofTBM performance, However,such a single value classification systemas thesummedvalueof.six parameters has restrictions in terms of the establishmentof anaccuratereliable correlation between the TBM performance factors and RMR system.In this case,all of theinput parameters of the classification system,i.e. UCS,RQD,joint spacingjoint condition, groundwater condition and adjustment factor for  joint orientation, identically influence the penetration rate.The correlation between the RMR and penetration rate assumes the same trend as correlations between the penetration rate and these six parameters.This inherent assumption will decrease the accuracy of empirical equations. Besides,as was noted by Zhao2007,input parameters of this rock mass classification were rated for rock mass characterization and relatedto supportdesign in classical tunneling they were not selected to describe rock mass boreability in TBM tunneling.

Keeping the above issues in mind,there existquestionable factors involved in the existing rock mass classification systems for TBM performance prediction

## 4 SUGGESTIONS REGARDING USE OFROCK MASS CLASSIFICATIONS INTBMPERFORMANCE PREDICTION

## 4.1 Multiple Variable Regression Analysis

RockMassRating RMRclassification system is determined from the sum of ratings weightings of input parameters:15for the uniaxial compressive strength of rock,20 for the RQD,20 for the joint spacing30 for the joint condition,and 15 for the groundwater condition with arithmetic summation of 100. As noted by Bieniawski1973,the RMR system was originally developed for calculation of rock load and tunnel support selection. Therefore, the given weightings are ingood agreement with the tunnel design process for given rock mass condition.The given ratings of the inputs, howeverdo not ensureto beappropriatef for different purposes.An efficicnt strategy to extend the applicability of RMR,or in general all the rating based classification systems therefore, is to adjust the weights (rating value of input parameters compatible with different applications.Khademi Hamidi et al.,2010 performed an adjustment of the weights through multiple and polynomial regression analyses to develop a rating system suitable for TBM performance prediction.For this purpose,input parameters of the RMR with strong correlation with TBM FPI were found and the prediction model developed based on theseparameters by using different coefficients and rating system. Meanwhile, the. angle betweentunnel axis and discontinuity planeswas included in the model as a substitution of the adjustment factor for discontinuity orientation in RMR. Among the five multiple linear,non-linear and polynomial regression analyses,the multilinear regression was the most logical and promising formula for FPI estimation as follows:

As seen in the above equation,although the joint spacing is one of the most important effectiveparameters inpredictingTBM pcnctration ratcit is not includcd in thc formula.This is because of multicollinearity in the developed model resulting from a high correlation between two parametersUCS and joint spacing.

## 4.3 Use of Rock Mass Classifications for Prediction of TBM Advance Rate

The TBM performance is ultimately transformed to the machine advance rate which is used for estimation of tunnel completion time and cost.The advance rate is calculatedfrom TBM penetration rate in conjunction with anticipated machine utilization. Hencein the literature several studies have been carried out to develop predictionmodels of TBM utilization or advance rate.Among them,attempts were made to correlate the advance rate with rock mass classification systemsBarton,2000 Alber,2000Sapigni et al.,2002Ribacchi andLembo-Fazio,2005Bieniawski and Grandori,2007.

Since the TBM utilizationis mainly affected by ground conditions, support requirement and cutter replacement and on the other hand,t the rock mass quality iS closely related to tunnel stability,based on which theses classification systemswere developed, 7hao (2007) noted that correlation Jo rock mass class to TBM utilization/advance rate is expected to be betterthan the correlation to TBM penetration rate. This is illustrated through a comparison of correlation of Rock Mass Excavability(RME system with TBM rate of penetration (ROP) and averagerateof advance ARA) on a database compiled from Gilgel Gibe II tunnel in Figure 7.

<!-- image -->

Scatter plot

Figure 7.Correlation of RME with TBM ROP and ARA(Gilgel Gibe II tunnel)

<!-- image -->

Line chart

The RME classification system was developed based on the RMR classification, similar to QtBM,which was developed based on the Q-system.As seen in the figure,TBM advance rate demonstrates stronger correlation to RME system (comparedto penetration rate).

## 5 CONCLUSIONS

Rock mass classification systems,may be useful tools for tunnel support design at the early stage of a project planning process. There are, however,a number of restrictions that should be applied if and when these systems are going to be used in TBM performance.prediction. Theapnlication potential of RMR system for predicting TBM penetration rate was illustrated in this paper. The results of the analysis revealed that the RMR system,or in general all the existing rating based rock mass classification systems with the present  scheme have l limited accuracyinTBMperformance prediction. Part of thc reason for such incompatibility is related to the fact that the parameters and their rating in these classifications are selected based on the relation with rock mass characterizationand support system,not TBM performance.

Followingtheaforementionedremarks, three recommendations are given, which could be applied on the existing rock mass classification systems in order their reliability inpredicting TBM performancetobe increased or could comprise the basis for the development of a new system.The limitation may be overcome by tuning up the rock mass classifications through adjusting the predefined ratings assigned tothe input parameters carried out by multiple variable regression analyses.The comparison of TBM FPI predicted using the RMR fabric index i.e.FRMR with measured FPI showed a high correlation coefficientproviding the rock mass fabric indices as alternative methodologies that can be used for prediction of TBM penetration rate.Also,a comparative analysis of relationship between Rock Mass Excavability (RME) indexwith .TBM penetration and advance rates showed that the RME is mostly correlated to TBM advance rate ratherthanthe penetrationrate.In summary,the authors strongly recommend the potential users of the RMR system should carefully study the limitations of this system as well as other classification systems they may want to apply,before taking them into use in performance prediction of TBM.

## Ground Volume Loss and Depth of Placement of the Tunnel Control in EPB TBMExcavation

## H.Karimnia

Shahrood University of technology, Shahrood, lran

ABSTRACT Analyses of ground volume loss in EPB TBM excavation are presented in this paper.In this article considering the information obtained from boreholes,ground settlement condition between the stations N.11 and 12 of Line 1 of Tabriz Subway has been investigated.Using Peck's empirical method,ground settlement,caused by tunnel excavation, has been calculated considering the different values for i,ground volume loss and the distance between tunnel axis and surface, then the transverse profile of ground settlement has been prepared. The results show that the increase in ground volume loss and decrease in the distance between tunnel axis and surface causes an increase in ground settlement value. Also a simultaneous increase in ground volume loss and the distance between tunnel axis and surface causes the increase of ground settlement proving the more important role of ground volume loss than the distance between tunnel axis and surface in ground settlement.

## 1INTRODUCTION

The increasing demandfor transport infrastructures in recent decades has resulted in considerable growthin underground works,especially inurbancnvironments where there is less surface space available. Mechanizedexcavation using EPB typc TBM's is particularly recommended in these contexts whereunstableface behavior must be tackled in ground with particularly poor geotechnical characteristics,in order to minimize effects at ground level and to work in hydrostatic conditions when below the water table Lunardi,2000.The control of deformation inducedin theground by excavation plays a very important role at the design stage for assessing the risk of damage to existing structures,such as buildings and underground utilities, as reliably as possible.

Empirical formulations are very useful for evaluating expected subsidence (Peck 1969, Rankin 1987,OReilly&amp; New 1982,Herzog experimental findings taken from a very 1985). Developed onthebasisof large number of case histories,these allow Gaussian subsidence curves to be quickly determined from simple parameters such as the geometrical data of the operation (tunnel diameterD and overburdenH the geotechnical characteristics of the ground by dcfininga cocfficientK, varying between 0.2 and 0.3for granular soils and between 0.4 and 0.7for cohesive soils and the method of excavation identificd by a value for expected ground loss V.

According to the above, one ofthe effective parameters on the surface settlement  are the overhead geometric

The value assumed for ground loss in the process described is without doubt the key parameter in the analysis and it is therefore fundamental that it is accurately forecast at the design stage and that it is monitored during the construction stage.

parameters or the placement depth of the tunnel.In the present study, the effect of the two factors are considered on the ground surface settlement between the N.11 and 12 stations of Line 1 of Tabriz Subway using Peck's empirical method.

## 2INTRODUCTIONTHEPROJECT

The study was carried out at the twin subway tunnels of first linc of Tabriz city,which is located at East Azerbaijan,Iran.It has the urban population of approximately 1,280,000 in2012ast thereis about 1,800,000 passengers travel inside the city every day.It is expected that the city population will be around2,000,000 and about4,500,000 passengers will travel each day inside the city in 2020.So considering the high volume of travels inside the city and the necessity of using the railway transport system in order to minimize the city Traffic problems,studies on the first line of subway were started in 2000.Tabriz railway network comprises 4 paths that are designed with 60 stations and the length of 60 km UURC,2007.The general layout of the metro of Tabriz is shown in Figure 1.

Figure 1.General layout of Tabriz metro (UURC,2007)

<!-- image -->

Geographical map

The first Line of Tabriz subway with the length of 60 km,links the southeast region to southwest.It is physically designed as onsurface,shallow, deep and high.Itis constructed as twin single way tunnels in deep section that is excavated with TBM and EPB machines with an excavation diameter of 6.88 m,an external diameter of segments of 2.18m and an internal diameter of tunnels of 6 m,so there is a distance of 14 cm between the excavation diameter and external diameter of segments that is filled with shot concrete. In this paper,the section between Stations N.11 and 12 of the first line of Tabriz Subway that contains old and traditional structures with low strength has been studied.In general,Tabriz is placed on the sedimentationsof third andfourth Geological periods that contain clay stone, sandstone,conglomerate, tuff,tuffitgyps and alluvial sedimentations such as clay, silt, sand,gravel and pebble of the various grade (TURO,2005).

## 3GROUND SURFACE SETTLEMENT ASARESULTOF EPBEXCAVATION

Due to the importance of subsidence in urban areas, in recent decades,using earth pressure balance shiclds for excavating tunnels has become common because of various reasons such as decreasing subsidence,increasing excavation speed and safety.

Tunneling machines developed settlement with the excavation and the progress in loose soils.The balanced excavation machines of ground pressure have a similar operation.

Because of ground pressure balance system, the working under the water level is possible.In the machine,the excavated soils to create a balance in the face are used and carried out excavation operations Hetrog 2005).

The excavation subsidence as a result of EPB machine can be seen in Figure 2.

## 5STUDY OF THE VOLUME LOSS EFFECT ONTHE GROUND SURFACE SETTLEMENT

The volume of the surface settlement trough, V,can be expressed as a percentage of the volume of excavation and is referred to as the percentage volume loss.

During excavationthe ground around tunnel can shift inward so that the stress relief occurs.

Therefore,it always is necessary that the excavated volume is the larger than the holes final volume.The added cxcavate volume is called the volume loss (Sweeney,2006.

For a TBM tunnel the volume loss is the sum of the following four components,the magnitude of which will depend upon the methodology and control of the construction, and the soil typeVittorio et al.,2007. Face loss:

This component is due to loss of ground intothe face of the excavation.The magnitude of face loss is influenced by the ground type and the support pressure applied to the faceFig.3.

Figure 3.Face loss

<!-- image -->

Engineering drawing

## Loss around the shield:

The excavated diameter of the tunnel is typically greater than the diameter of the tail skin of the shield.This leaves an annulus around the shicld into which the surrounding ground can move.The magnitude of the resulting volume loss is dependent on the stability of the ground around the tunnel Fig.45.

Figure 4.Loss of radial shields

<!-- image -->

Engineering drawing

Figure 5.Loss overall radial shields

<!-- image -->

Engineering drawing

## Loss behind the shield

As the TBM moves forwards an annular space is let around the tunnel lining.This will be injected with grout to reduce the associated volume loss.The magnitude of volume loss depends on the effectiveness of the grout injection,the grouting pressure and the time for the grout to gain sufficient strength to prevent ground movementFig. 6)

Figure 6.Loss behind the shield

<!-- image -->

Engineering drawing

## 7EFFECTOFTUNNELDEPTHON GROUND SURFACE SETTLEMENT CURVE

Consideringt the tunnels excavation depth vary between 17 to 23 meters at the N.11 and N.i2 stations of Line 1 of Tabriz Subway the ground surface settlementprofile is considered in the various depths H and values of constant volume loss.The results arc shown at Figurcs 11 to 14.

The results of ground subsidence curves for different values of V and the tunnel depth are presented in Table 4.As can be seen, the value of the surface settlement increased with increase the volume loss and constant the tunnel depth.

Ground longitudinal subsidence diagrams are plotted for different values of the tunnel depth and the volume loss 1% in Figure 15. As it can be observed,almost in all depths in distance of 2.4 times the diameter of the tunnel subsidence reaches stationary state.

## 8 CONCLUSIONS

The value forground loss constitutes a key parameter for managing excavation using an EPB shield,because of its importance in assessing surface subsidence phenomena and building damage risk.Itis the sumof different components,which develop during the stages of tunnel advance at the face and around the cavity,in the tail zone and after the preceded segments lining is placed.The importance impact of this issuc and depth of placement of the tunnel onto ground surface settlement was discussed in this paper.

The value of the ground surface settlement between N.11 and N.12 stations of Linc 1 of Tabriz Subway for the tunnel depth of 17, 19,21 and 23 meters and the volume loss of 0.8,1.1.3 and 1.5 studied.The Results Table 4.Results ground surface settlement using by Peck's empiricalmethodfor different value of Vand tunnel depth showed that the ground subsidence increased with increasethevolume loss due to mechanized tunneling. The lower depth the tunnel is excavated,the less the effect of the tunnel excavation is observed.

## Cift Kalkanli TBM ile Kazilan Yamanli II HES Projesi 1. Kademe Enerji Tunelinde Calisma Performansi

Working Performance in the Irt Stage Energy Tunnel of Yamanli II HEPP Project Excavated by a Double Shield TBM

## S.Yilmaz

Enerjisa Enerji A.$.,Ankara

## i.Avci

Temelsu Uluslar arasi Mihendislik HizmetleriA.S.,Ankara

## i.Gney,M.Akgl

NTF Insaat Ltd.$ti.,Ankara

OZET Bu calismada,Enerjisa'nin Yamanli II HES Projesi kapsaminda,4.305 mm kazi capli cift kalkanli bir TBM ile kazisi ve yapimi devam eden 1.Kademe Enerji Tineli'nde calisma performansi net kazi hizlar,gunluk ve aylik ilerlemeler,makine kullanim oranlari ve duraklamalarzetlenecektir.Tunel uzunlugu 6.618m'dir.Yüklenici firma NTF kazi islerinin basladigt 27Ekim 2012 tarihinden 2013Haziran sonuna kadar 2.973m tünel imalatini bitirmistir. Her projede oldugu gibi,ilk bir kac ayda yasanan ögrenme periyodundaki dusuk performansdan sonra,proje ckibin tecrube kazanmasi ve ciddi bir egitim sirecinden sonra yuksek performanslara ulasilmistur.Makine kullanim oranlari%60'lara ve aylik ilerlemeler 730 metrelere ulasmistir.

ABSTRACT Working performance (instantaneous penetration rate,daily and monthly advance rates,machine utilization timeands stoppages in the lst Stage Energy Tunnel of Enerjisa Yamanli II HEPP Project excavated by a double shield TBM with 4.305 mm excavation diameter is summarized.The length of tunnel is 6.618 m.The contractor .company NTF completed construction of the 2.973m tunnel starting from 27October 2012until the end of June 2013.After low performance in a few months of the learning period as in encountered in every project,higher performance parameters were obtained as a result of gaining experience of the project team and a serious education period. Machine utilization time reached up to 65% and monthly advance rate reached up to 730 m.

## 1GiRIS

Ulkelerin refah seviyesi arttikca enerji sektörinde meydana gelen buyime ekonomik buyumeden dahafazladir.Enerji sektoru ülkemizde ekonomik büyümeden 2ila 2,5 kat daha fazla büyümektedir.Türkiye'de enerji sektörü 2012 yilmda kurulu guc olarak %8,1 oraninda. buyumustur.Bu..oran Avrupa Birligi'nde yer alan bazi ülkelerin i 6 yillik büyümesine denktir. Olkemiz tüm dinamikleriyle bunu bir yilda gerceklestirmistir.

ikemizin enerjide olan disa bagimliligini azaltmak amaciyla,HES projelerine hiz verilmistir.Son 10 yilda ozel sektorun enerji piyasasina dahil olmasiyla enerji scktoru buyuk ivme kazanmustur. Su an itibariyle 20.069 MW kurulu guc ile yilda yaklasik 71 milyar kWh hidroelektrik enerji uretir hale gelinmistir.Bu onemli bir rakam olmasina ragmen Turkiye'yeyetmemektedir. Suyun gucünden daha fazla istifade etmemiz, zaman kaybetmeden potansiyelimizi kullanip, enerjide disa olan bagumliligimizien aza indirmemiz icin yapimi srmekte olan 1.495 adet projenin hayata gecirilmesi ile yillik uretimimiz110.000 GWholacaktur.Bu projeler tamamladiginda,Türkiye enerjide kendine yeten, daha gelismis birulke olacaktir.Ulkemizin sürekli artan nüfusunun ihtiyacini karisilayacak enerji uretim projelerinde,daha hizl ve daha ckonomik tüinel imalati saglayan TBM'lerin kullanmt ginimizde oldukca artmistir ve de artmaya devam edecegi ongorulmektedir.

Bucalismada Enerjisa'ninYamanli II HESProjesi kapsaminda,4.305 mm kazi sapli cift kalkanl bir TBM ile kazisi ve yapimi yuklenici NTF tarafindan devam eden 6.618 m uzunlugundaki 1.Kademe Enerji Tüneli'ndecalismaperformansi netkazi hizlarigunlük ve ayhk ilerlemeler,makine kullanim oranlari ve duraklamalar) özetlenecektir.

## 2.1 Proje Hakkinda Genel Bilgiler

Yamanli II HES Projesi,Akdeniz bolgesinin dogu kesimlerinde olup,360730-36 1500kuzey enlemleri ile 375230 380130dogu boylamlar arasinda yer - almaktadir.Yamanli IIHES Proje alan, Adanaili Saimbeyliilcesinin kus nsnon yaklasik 13 km kuzeydogusu ile14km guncydogusu arasinda, Seyhan nchrinin kolu olanGoksuirmagi boyunca uzanan saha icinde yer almaktadir Proje sahasi Adana'nin 150 km kuzey dogusundadir $ek.1.

Dsi Genel Müdürligu tarafindan VerbundPlan, Romconsult veTemelsu firmalar grubuna hazirlatilan ve Eylül 1984 tarihinde yayinlanan "Yukari Seyhan Havzasi Master Plan Raporunda,Seyhan nchrinin iki ana kolundan birisi olan Goksu irmagiüzerinde, membadanmansabadogru. YamanliI, Yamanl II ve Yamanli III regülatorleri ile

Seyhan Nehri'nin en onemli kollarmdan birisi olan Goksu Nehri üzerinde ki Yamanli I Projesinde iki kademe ile enerji üretimi planlanmistir.

Feke, Menge ve Koprü barajlari planlanmistir.

2. KademeProje GoksuIrmagi'nda Yamanli-II 2.Kademe Regulatoru4.180 m uzunlukta basinch enerji tüneli ile akedük, denge bacasi, cebri boru ve sol sahilde Yamanl HES.

Yamanli-II 1.Kademe Regulatoru ve HES (memba Projesi enerji amachidir ve drenaj alan 1.693 kmdir.Projenin icerdigi kisimlar sunlardir:

- 1.Cevirme Yapisi
- 3.Derivasyon Yapisi (Tuneli)

1.Kademe kapsaminda bulunan 9.311m uzunlugundakienerji tünelininhizlibir sekilde tamamlanmasi, projenin ekonomikligi acisindan onemli olmasinedeniyle TBM kullanimina karar verilmistir.

Kullanilan beton kaplama, segment dizaym Universal tiptedir,5 adet segment ve 1 adet key. segmentile ringolusturulmaktadir. Segment dis capi 4000 mm,ic capi 3500 mm ve uzunlugu 1350 mm'dir.Su sizdirmalarina karsi segmentler arasinda lastik contalar kullanilmistur. Gerekli ekipmanlarla ringlerin birbirine monte edildikten sonra,zeminle segmentarasindaki bosluk pea-gravel ye enjeksiyon ile doldurulmaktadir.

Tunel acma makinclerinin ilk yatirim ve isletmemaliyetleri oldukca pahali olmasi nedeni ile kazi ve desteklemenin yapilacagi formasyonlarda .yapilan ek etütlerin tanimlanmasi sonucunda,kazi ve destekleme islerinin9+311-6+950arasi klasik delmepatlatma yontemiyleve6+950=0+317,2 arasi cift kalkanh TBM ile yapilmasi karalastirilmistir.

Pasa nakli icin 4adet tren katari vardir. Her bir tren katari 1 adet lokomotif 4 adet yuk vagonuher biri 8mkapasiteli,2 adet segment arabasi,1 adet tasiyici araba,1 adet pea-gravel arabasi ve 1 adet personel tasiyici aracindanolusmaktadir.Kazilanmalzeme portal cikisindaki doküm sahasina bosaltilmaktadir.

Projenin kaziyabaslamatarihi olarak belirlenen03.08.2012 tarihinden itibaren 470 gun icinde16.11.2013tarihinekadar bitirilmesi planlanmistir.

## 2.2 Tünel Güzergahinin Jeolojisi

3,50mcap ve6.618muzunlugunda planlanmis olan ilctim tüneli,projenin en zorlu ve problemli kismini olusturmaktadir.

Sol.sahileprojelendirilmisolantunel guzergahi,onemli bir yapisal fay hattina (Kaleboynu Fayiparalel gitmektedir.Soz konusu fay Kaleboynu Koyü'nden kuzeye dogru genc birimler tarafindan örtilü oldugu icin izlenememekte ancak varligi bilinmcktcdir.Tünel guzergahinin yapisal bir süreksizlige paralel gitmesi,tunel acilmasi sirasinda hic istenmeyen kosullardan birisidir. Bu kosullar, surekli olarak duraylilik,ani su geliri vb sorunlar delgi islemi sirasinda beklemelere ve aksamalara sebep olabilecektir.

Tunel,0+200-6+900 arasinda Koroglu ve Simbüldagi Formsyonlarinda bulunan Neretik Kiractasi birimlerinden gececektir. Formasyonlar az ve orta ayrismislardir yakin aralikli6-20cmve orta aralikli20-60 cmdogrultuveegimi250/73-300/66= 130/74 90/6 olansureksizlikler icermektedirlerortalamaRQD%79'dur. Ortu tabakasi kalinligi ortalama 400mdir. Litolojik birimlerin basinc dayanimlar 50 ile 100 MPa arasinda degismektedir.Damlama ve..sizinti mertebesinde ..yeralti suyu beklenmektedir, ancak kirectasinin karstik zelliginden dolayi yer yer dere gecislerinde su bosalimi olmast buyuk bir olasiliktir. Formasyonlarin Q kaya sinifi degeri ortalama 10,53 ve RMR degeri 65-70 civarindadir.

## 2.3 Cift Kalkanl TBM'in Ozellikleri

YamanlII.1.Kademe EnerjiTuneli insaatinda 4,305 m kazi capli Robbins marka Cift Kalkanl Tunel Acma Makinesi (TBM kullanulmaktadir Sek.2).TBMkurulu gücu 1.870 kWtr.Kesici kafa 4x330 kW elektrik motorlar iledizaynedilmistir.Gripper pabuclarin yan duvara basarakkendisini sabitleyip ve itme silindirleri ile aynaya basarak kesici kafa kazi yapmaktadir. Ayrica, yardimciauxiliaryhidroliksilindirler segmente basarak tek kalkan modunda (single shield mode) kazi yapabilmektedir.

TBM kesici kafasinda bulunan 28 adct17 inc432mm capindakidisklerzemini keserek pasa nakil hattinaulastirir.Cift kalkanli makine kullanildigindan dolayi kazi sürekli olarak (kazi ve tahkimat isleri ayn anda) gerceklesebilmektedir. ...Kullanilan TBM'in genel teknik özellikleri Cizelge 1'de Ozetlenmektedir.

Aksamalarin gozlenmesitaninin dogru konmasi amaciyla gunluk imalatraporlari titizlikle ikarilmaldir.Günluk imalat raporlarininverileri cercevesindeaksayan kisimlar revizyonlarla giderilmistir.

- 1.Kademe enerji tuneli 6+935,566 ve 0+317,200 km arasi planlanan ve gerceklesen aylik kazi ve destekleme isleri Cizelge 2'de ozetlenmektedir.Ortalama aylikilerleme hizlarinin 2013 Ocak ayinda179mden 2013 Mayis ayinda 727m'ye ulastig gorulmektedir (Sek.3).

TBM tireten firmanin programlanan sürede montaji bitirememesi sonucu yüklenicinin organizasyonunu ve is programini etkilemistir.

2013yilnin ilk 6ayindagerceklesen ortalamavemaksimumgunlukilerleme özetlenmektedir.Ortalamagunlükilerleme hizlarinin 2013Ocak ayinda 5,8 m'den 2013 Mayis ayinda 23,5 m'ye ulastigi gorulmektedir Sekil 4.Ortalama net kazi hizlarinin sadece kazi yapilan sire icindeki ilerleme hizi,duraklamalar haric ise 1,2734 ile.2,5590 m/saatarasinda degistigi görülmektedir.

köpük püskürtme sistemi killerin sivanmasi nedeniyle sik sik tikanmis ve calismalarda buyukzaman kayiplarinaneden olmustur. Tünel boyunun uzamasina bagli olarak olusan arizalaringiderilmesi icin lojistik destek saglamak zaman almaktadir.

Ocak 2013de secilen trafo sistemi ile TBMin.kullandig guc.arasindaki uyumsuzluklarin.oldugu gorulmüs ve revizyona gidilmistir.

Tinel yapim esnasinda karsilasilan cesitli duraklama nedenleri vesüreleri,bakum surelerimakinekullanimsurelerive yizdeleriay bazinda Cizelge 4de &amp;zetlenmcktedir.Makine kullanim orant,bir vardiyaicindekaziya ayrilan zamanin yüzdesidir, diger zamanlarda cesitli nedenlerleplanl-bakamveplansiz-ariza duraklamalarolmaktadir.Gorüldugugibi makine kullanim oran %35 ile %61 arasinda arasinda degismistir,ortalama %49 olmustur. Planli duraklamalarin (bakim orani%18,6ve dolayisiile.plansizduraklamalarinorani %32.4olmustur.

Subat 2013de segment vinci arizalari nedeniyle revizyona gidilmistir.Erektor ve PLC ayarlari zaman kaybina neden olmustur.

Nisan2013ayinda Kaliforniyamakasi montaji yapilmistir. Enjeksiyon islemleri (pea gravel+mortar+kontakkazrhizinauyum saglamasi icin ck onlemler alinip kontak enjeksiyonunun yapilabilmesi icin ek iki adet gentry sisteme ilave edilmistir.

Mart 2013 ayi sonunda karstik bosluklu ve killi yapilarin kazi aynasindagorlmeye baslamasikazivedesteklemeislerinin yapimini zorlastirmis,kafatemizligi-bakim zamanlarinin artmasina neden olmustur.

Mayis 2013 ayinda ideale yakin bir kazi ve destekleme hizi yakalanmistir. Kesici degisimleri ve erektor arizalari mayis ayimin zaman kayiplarini olusturmustur.Bakimin gin icerisindeki saatleri degistirilmistir.

Haziran 2013 ayi icerisinde Kaliforniya makasimin yeri degistirilip TBM e yaklasturilmistir.Ayrica formasyon icindeki killi malzemenin cogalmasi nedeniyle kafanin kil ile sivanmasi temizlik icin gerekli olan zamanin artmasina neden olmustur. Kesicilerin killi formasyondan formasyona gecisleriesnasindakirilmalar yasanmistir. Kesici kafada kullanilan su- Cizelge 5 ve $ekil 5'de planlanan ve gerceklesen kazi isleri özetlenmektedir.

## Kazilabilirlik indeksi Kullanilarak Tünel Acma Makinasi (TBM) Performans Parametrelerinin Belirlenmesi

Analysis of the TBM Performance Parameters Using Boreability Index

CemalBalci,Ramazan Comakl,Can Polat,Faraz Shoaee Sarbangholi lstanbul Teknik niversitcsi,Maden Mithendisligi Bolimi,lstanbul

OZET Performans tahmini ve tünel acma makinelerinin (TBM) performans parametrelerinin belirlenmesi,mekanize kaziprojelerinin ön projelendirmeasamasinda kullanilan kritik paramctrelcr oldugundan tünclcilik cndüstrisi icin cok onemlidir. Bu bildiride istanbul'da yapilan iki farkli uygulama,detayli arazi ve laboratuvarcalismalariyla analiz edilmistir. Tunel acma makineleri (TBM icin performans parametreleri(baski kuvveti, tork ve spesifik enerji degerleri tunel kazist boyunca toplanmis,TBM'lerin tunellerin kazismdaki performans sonuclari özetlenmistir.Nokta yuk dayanimi deneyleri tunel kazilari sirasinda araziden alinan laboratuvarinda yapilmis ve tek eksenli basinc dayanimi degerleri nokta yük dayanimi deney sonuclarindan elde edilmistir.

Yapilan bu calismada laboratuar deney sonuclarr ve TBM arazi verileri birlikte degerlendirilmis ve Gong ve dig.2006,tarafindan önerilen spesifik kaya kutle kazilabilirlik kazilabilirliginin tünel acma makinalarinin degerlendirilmesinde kullanilabilecegi sonucu elde edilmistir.

ABSTRACT Performance prediction and the determination of some design parameters of TBMs are becoming very crucial for tunneling industry since they are critical elements in the pre-planning of a mechanical excavation project. In this paper three case studies are analyzed with a detailed field and laboratory studies concerming inIstanbul.Theperformance parameters of the tunnel boring machines Cutter forces,i.e. thrust forcerolling force,and specific energy values) are also collected during the excavation of the tunnels.Tunnel boring machine's performance results of the tunnels are summarized. Field data are collected carefully in order to compare TBM operational parameters such as field thrust,torque,net cutting rate,specific energy and boreability index. Point Load Strength and Shore Hardness tests were carried out at the rock excavation laboratories of the ITU Mining Engineering Department on the rock samples collected during the excavation of the tunnels.Uniaxial compressive strength was determined by Point Load Strength.

It is shown in this paper that the boreability index at penetration 1 mm/rev.,defined by Gong et al.2007 as specific rock mass boreability index(SRMBI is a good indicator of the rock mass properties and may be used to evaluate the rock mass borcability for TBM in Turkey.

## 1GiRiS

Tinel acma makinalarTBM projelerinin dogru sekilde planlanabilmesi icin kayacin kazilabilirliginin arastirilip yorumlanmasi gerekmektedir. Howarth (1987) kazilabilirligi, kaya kütlesi icerisinde mekanize kazi makinasinin ilerleme miktarinin tahmin edilmesi olarak aciklamaktadir.Yine baska bir tanimlamada kazilabilirlik,uygulanan bir itmekuvveti altinda belirli sayida ve t tipteki keskilerin kayac özelliklerine bagli olarak ilerleme miktar... olarak tanimlanmaktadir (US Commission on Engineering and Technical Systems,1984.Kazilabilirlikkonusunda Gong ve dig.2006 tarafindan onerilen kaya kütle ..kazilabilirlik indeksinin, kaya kutlesininkazilabilirliginin yorumlanmasi konusundaBalci2009tarafindan. bir calisma yapilmis ve kaya kütle kazilabilirlik indeksinin guvenli bir parametre oldugu ortaya konulmustur.

Bu calismada Gong ve dig. 2006 tarafindanonerilen spesifik kaya kutle kazilabilirlik indeksidegerleri kullanilarak ikifarklprojedekullanilan TBM'lerin performanslari analiz edilmistir.

## 2 TUNELPROJELERiNiN TANITILMASI

## 2.1 Projelerde Kullanilan TBM'ler ve Ozellikleri

Ayazaga-Cayirbasi su nakliye tuneli, Istanbul Buyuksehir Belediyesi (tsKi) tarafindan Kagithane vadisine temizsu tedarik edilmesi amaciyla yapilmistir. Tunel insaati Depar insaat sirketitarafindan yapilmis olup 4,750 m uzunlugunda ve 2,2m capinda tinelacilmistir.Insaatsirasinda kalkanl sert kaya TBMi kullanilmistir. $ekil I'de bu TBM gosterilmistir.

Kadikoy-Kozyatagituneli 17Agustos 2012'de kullanima acilmstur.Bu tunel 21,7 km uzunlugunda ve toplam 16 istasyondan olusmaktadir.Tinelin acilmasi islemlerinde Kadikoy-Kozyatagi ve Kartal-Kaynarca arasinda iki adet pasa basincli EPBTBM kullanilmistir. iki projede kullanilan

## 3TUNELLERiNJEOLOJiLERi

Ayazaga tunelindeki kaya formasyonu,genel olarak Trakya formasyonu olarak bilinen ve kumtasi, camurtasi veandezit vedasit dayklar gibi sedimanter kaya icerisinde bulunan magmatik kayaclardan olusmaktadir (Anadolu,2006).

Ayrica kazi hattinda Alüvyonlar ve fay hatlani dabulunmaktadir.Kozyatagi Kadikoy kazi hattinin gececegi jeolojik yapilarn durumunu gosterir kesit Sekil 2de verilmistir.

Kadiköy-Kartal metro tinelinin Kozyatagi Kadiköy arasindaki kazi islemleri TBM'lerin performans parametreleri acisindan degerlendirilmistir.Degerlendirilen bu kazi hattrTrakya,Tuzla-Baltaliman ve Kartal formasyonlarindan gecmektedir.

$ekil 2.Kadiköy Kartal Metro tüneli guzergahi (Balci,2009)

<!-- image -->

Engineering drawing

Kozyatagi -Kartalmetro hattinngectigi formasyon oldukca kirikli-catlakl olmakla birlikte, sedimanter kayaclar icerisinde aniden olusan andezit ve dasit dayklar gibi magmatik kayaclarda formasyon icerisinde yer almaktadir.Bu dayklarin kalinlig 10-20 cm'den 10-11 m'ye kadar degismektedir Ozgorüs ve Okay.2005).

Kesici kafanin her bir donüsteki ilerleme miktari,donme hizi, tork,ilerleme miktari ve itme kuvveti gibiTBM performans parametreleri makine üzerindeki veri kayit sistemi tarafindan kaydedilmis ve bu veriler analiz edilmistir.Elde edilen bu veriler Cizelge 2 ve 3de verilmistir.Cizelge 2 ve 3deki veriler her bir ilerleme degeri icin verilerin ortalamasi alinarak elde edilmistir.

Ayazaga=Cayirbasitunelindenalinan orneklerüzerindeyapilan noktayük dayanimi testi sonuclarindan hesaplanan tek eksenli basincdayanumidegerlerinin30250 MPa arasinda, Kadikoy=Kozyatagi tinelindenalinan numuneler üzerinde yapilan tek eksenlibasinc dayanimi testi sonucunda ise tek eksenli basinc dayanimi degerlerinin 30-65MPa arasinda degistigi gözlemlenmistir.

## 4TBMPERFORMANS PARAMETRELERININ HESAPLANMASI

Tünel kazilarsirasinda düzenliolarak müteahhit firma yetkilileri tarafindan TBM'lerin kazi performans verileri kayst altina alinmistir. Bu calisma icin alinan verilerin timu TBM acik modda calistigi sirada kaydedilen verilerden olusmaktadir.

Elde edilen bu veriler degerlendirilerek, Kazilabilirlik indeksi degeri bir disk zerine düsen arazi itme kuvveti(kN/disk degerinin kesicikafaninbir..donüsteki ilerleme miktarina (mm/dev) orani olarak hesaplanmustir.Kaziicin harcanan guc Esitlik -1den,arazi net kazi miktarr ICR ise ilerleme miktari ve tunel kazi arininin alani kullanilarak hesaplanmitur.Spesifik enerji SFdegeri net arazi kazi miktar (ICR)ve kazi icin harcanan guce bagli olarak hesaplanmis olup,enerji transfer katsayisi (k) 0.85olarak ele alinmistir Balci,2009Bilgin ve dig.2007.

## 5SONUCLAR

Yapilan bu calismada Ayazaga su tüneli ve daha kirikli-catlakli bir formasyonda acilan Kadiköy-Kozyatagi metro calismalariarazi verileri Gong vedig.2007tarafindan gelistirilen kazilabilirlik indeksi kullanilarak degerlendirilmistir.

Sonuc olarak kazilabilirlik indeksinin kaya kütle ozelliklerinin iyi bir gostergesi oldugu kaya kazilabilirliginin ve degerlendirilmesinde kullanilabilecegi ortaya cikmistir.

N.Bilgin iTMaden Fakiltesi, Maden Mih.Bol., Maslak,lstanbul

OZET Kadiköy Kartal Metrosu'nun Kadiköy ile Kozyatagi arasinda kalan toplam 12,3 km'lik ikiz hat tunellerinin acilmasinda,acik modda da calisabilen ozellikte EPB TBM'ler kullanilmistir Gecilen kayaclar, Kartal Formasyon'una ait kirectasi ve $eyller iyi-zayif kaya kutle kaliteli, Trakya formasyon'una ait kiltast, silttasi ve kiltasi zayif -cok zayif kaya ktle kalitesine sahip olup fay ve volkanik sokulumlar ile siklikla kesilmistir.Bu tiinellerin kazisinda,fay ve volkanik sokulumlarin gecilmesi sirasinda toplam 11g gocuk meydana gelmis bu gocukler kazi ilerlemesinin toplam 93 gun,4 saat sureyle duraksamasina sebep olmustur.Bu calismada TBMkaziparametrelerinin izlenmesi ve yorumlanmasiyla göcuk olaylarinin onceden tahmin edilebilmesi incelenmistir

ABSTRACT EPB TBMs,which are able to work in open mode,were used during the excavation works of the twin line tunnels between Kadiköy and Kozyatagi of Kadikoy-Kartal Metro Project that has a total length of 12,3 km.Excavated rocks are consist of limestone and shale -good-weak rock mass quality-belongs to Kartal Formation and siltstone and claystone -weak-very weak rock quality-belongs to Trakya Formation, which are cut by faults and volcanic intrusions.During mass the excavation,11 collapses occurred while passing through these faults and volcanic intrusions and these collapses caused stoppages that have a total length of 93 days and 4 hours. In this study, predictability of these collapses is examinedl by monitoring and interpreting TBM excavation parameters.

## 1 GiRi$

Jeolojik kosullar her tünel projesinde farklidir ve kendine ozeldir.Bu nedenle TBM kazi performansi ile jeolojik kosullarin olusturdugu olumsuzluklarin sistematik bir iliskisi dogrudan kurulamamaktadir.Ote yandan fay zonu gibi makro süreksizlik yapilari jeolojik nedenlerden kaynaklanan olumsuzluk-gecikmelerin en basinda gelmektedir.Proje. oncesinde ve sirasinda yapilan jeolojik calismalarin yogunlugu ve kalitesinin artmasiyla ve TBM secimi ve tasariminin bu calismalardan elde edilecekverilerinisigi altinda ayapilmasiyla jcolojik nedenlerden dolayi ortaya cikacak olumsuzluklari azaltmak mümkündür.Ancak, EPB TBM'lerde kazilan ortam tanimlayabilmek icin ayna yuzeyini gozle görmeközellikle basinclicalisildigi zaman oldukca zor vc zaman alici olup,basinc altinda calisabilecek özel kisiler tarafindan yapilabilir. Bu durum ile bas etmek icin ilerleme sirasinda elde edilen TBM kazi parametrelerini izlemek ve degerlendirmek uygunbiryol olarak gözükmektedir Bilginvd 2009Bu calismada Kadiköy-Kartal Metrosu'nun Kozyatagi-Kadiköy arasindaki hat tünellerinin kazisi sirasindaki göcuk olaylan incelenmis, TBM kazi parametrelerinin\_izlenmesi ve yorumlanmasiyla göcuklerin onceden kestirilebilmesi imkani ele alinmistir.

## 2KADIKOY KARTAL METROSU

## 2.1 Güzergah ve Proje Karakteristikleri

Kadiköy-Kartal Metrosu,Kadiköy ile Kartal arasinda 21,6 km uzunlugunda planlanmis olup,.gzergah i boyunca 16 istasyon icermektedir.Proje,Kadikoymeydaninda yeralan istasyon ve buna bagli kuyruk tünelleri ile baslamakta ve Yeldegirmeni semtinin altindanilerleyerekibrahimaga istasyonu'naulasmaktadir.Metro hatti bu istasyonda Istabul'un Avrupa yakasini Anadolu yakasimna baglayan banliyö sistemi olan Marmaray projesi ile kesismektedir. Ibrahimaga Istasyonu'ndan sonra E-5 adiyla bilinen D-100 devlet karayolu izdüsümüne gelen hat Acibadem Köprusu'nden itibaren bu karayoluna hemen hemen paralel olarak ilerlemektedir.Metro hattiüzerindeki istasyonlar, sistemin dikine yolcu transferlerinin kolayca yapilabilmesine imkan verecek sekilde E-5 karayolunu kesen kavsak noktalarinda ya yakininda planlanmistir(Sek.1).

Tünellerdc örtu kalinligr 8-60 m arasinda degismekte olup ortalama kalinlik 30 m civarindadir.Tunel derinligi,topografyaya bagli olmakla beraber, biryandan metro sistemininazami tirmanma egimine diger yandanda istasyon derinligine bagli olmaktadir.

## 2.2 Güzergah Jeolojisi ve Formasyonlarin Karakteristikleri

TBM kazilari sirasinda kazi sirasinagore baslica Kartal, Baltalimani, Tuzla ve Trakya formasyonlarindan gecilmektedir. Bu formasyonlarasagida kisaca tanimlanmis olup, ayrica $ekil 2'de gosterilmistir.

Kartal Formasyonu,sarimsi kahverengi, laminal, seyrek olarak silttasi ve kumtasi ara tabakali,.... fosilli seyl ve kirectasi seviyelerinden olusmaktadir. $eyl tabakalarinin. bulundugu kisimlar zayif dayanimli,catlakli-cok sik catlakli olmasina karsin,.. kirectasi tabakalarinin agirlikta oldugu kisimlari ise daha az catlakli ve göreli olarakdaha dayanimlidir. Guzergahta Hasanpasa Köprüsu (km 3+800) civarindan baslamakta,. Bostancideresinde (km 11+000yerini arkozlardan olusan Kurtköy formasyonuna birakmaktadir.

Tuzla Formasyonu yumrulu kircctaslari, cok ince tabakali-laminali 1 camurtaslari ile ardalanmali olarak bulunmaktadir. Baltalimani Formasyonu olarak adlandirilan tabakalar fosfatyumrulu,cortlu seyllerden olusmaktadir. Cokince tabakali olmasi nedeniyle cok kivrml bir karakteristik yapi gosterir.Tuzla Formasyonu ve Baltaliman Formasyonu güzergahta sadece Acibadem Istasyonu cevresinde (km 2+800-km 3+300) bulunmaktadir.

<!-- image -->

Table

Trakya Formasyonu,yesilimsi,gri renkli, yer yer cakiltasi ve daha cok kumtasi-silttasikiltasi ardalanmasi seklinde gözlenmektedir. Ortasaglam kaya özelligindeki Trakya Formasyonu,cogunlukla üc veya daha fazla catlak takimi icermektedir.Catlak aralari kil, kalsit ve yer yer kuvars dolguludur. Trakya Formasyonu Proje baslangici olan km 0+000 ile km 2+800 araliginda rastlanmaktadir.

Tünel güzergahinda bulunan formasyonlarin cogu degisik jeolojik devirlerde meydana gelen tektonik yer harcketleri sonucu ilkselkonumlarini kaybetmis,kayaclarin dayanum ve tabaka kalinlgina baghi olarak deforme olmus, kivrimli kirikli yapi kazanmistir. Güzergah boyunca rastlanilan kayaclarda,bu hareketlerin yönlerine bagl olarak genellikle üc veya daha fazla catlak sistemi gelismistir. Bunun.yanisira. asiri deformasyonlarin kivrimlanma ile karsilanamadigi zonlarda büyuk kiriklar-faylar ve ezilme zonlari meydanagetirmistir. Genellikle volkanik sokulumlarlabirlikte gözlenen bu. tür yapilarla güzergah boyunca siklikla karsilasilmaktadir.

Güzergahtaki alüvyonel dolgular,yüzey topografyasinin dahadüsük kota sahip oldugu dere ve vadi yapilarinin akarsu getirim malzemeleri ile doldurulmasi sonucu olusmustur.Bu formasyonlara sadece km 3+850 ile km 4+100 arasinda karsilasilmaktadir. Güzergahta gecilen formasyonlarin jeomekanik buyuklükleri Cizelge 1'de ozetlenmistir.Cizelge 1'den anlasilacagi uzere Trakya\_Formasyonu ve Kartal Formasyonu Zon A'da karsilasilan kayaclarin kaliteleri az cok biribirine benzer
